Axiom 1: mult(X,ld(X,Y)) = Y
Axiom 2: mult(rd(X,Y),Y) = X
Axiom 3: mult(mult(Z,mult(X,X)),Y) = mult(mult(Z,X),mult(X,Y))
Axiom 4: rd(mult(X,Y),Y) = X
Axiom 5: ld(X,mult(X,Y)) = Y

Lemma 6: mult(mult(rd(X,mult(Y,Y)),Y),mult(Y,Z)) = mult(X,Z)
Proof:
  mult(mult(rd(X,mult(Y,Y)),Y),mult(Y,Z))
= { by axiom 3 R->L }
  mult(mult(rd(X,mult(Y,Y)),mult(Y,Y)),Z)
= { by axiom 2 }
  mult(X,Z)

Lemma 7: mult(X,ld(Y,Z)) = mult(mult(rd(X,mult(Y,Y)),Y),Z)
Proof:
  mult(X,ld(Y,Z))
= { by lemma 6 R->L }
  mult(mult(rd(X,mult(Y,Y)),Y),mult(Y,ld(Y,Z)))
= { by axiom 1 }
  mult(mult(rd(X,mult(Y,Y)),Y),Z)

Lemma 8: mult(rd(X,mult(X,X)),X) = rd(Y,Y)
Proof:
  mult(rd(X,mult(X,X)),X)
= { by axiom 4 R->L }
  rd(mult(mult(rd(X,mult(X,X)),X),Y),Y)
= { by lemma 7 R->L }
  rd(mult(X,ld(X,Y)),Y)
= { by axiom 1 }
  rd(Y,Y)

Lemma 9: mult(rd(X,X),Y) = Y
Proof:
  mult(rd(X,X),Y)
= { by lemma 8 R->L }
  mult(mult(rd(rd(Y,X),mult(rd(Y,X),rd(Y,X))),rd(Y,X)),Y)
= { by axiom 2 R->L }
  mult(mult(rd(rd(Y,X),mult(rd(Y,X),rd(Y,X))),rd(Y,X)),mult(rd(Y,X),X))
= { by lemma 6 }
  mult(rd(Y,X),X)
= { by axiom 2 }
  Y

Lemma 10: rd(X,ld(Y,X)) = Y
Proof:
  rd(X,ld(Y,X))
= { by axiom 1 R->L }
  rd(mult(Y,ld(Y,X)),ld(Y,X))
= { by axiom 4 }
  Y

Lemma 11: mult(X,Y) = ld(mult(rd(Z,mult(X,X)),X),mult(Z,Y))
Proof:
  mult(X,Y)
= { by axiom 5 R->L }
  ld(mult(rd(Z,mult(X,X)),X),mult(mult(rd(Z,mult(X,X)),X),mult(X,Y)))
= { by lemma 6 }
  ld(mult(rd(Z,mult(X,X)),X),mult(Z,Y))

Lemma 12: mult(X,ld(Y,Z)) = ld(mult(rd(Y,mult(X,X)),X),Z)
Proof:
  mult(X,ld(Y,Z))
= { by lemma 11 }
  ld(mult(rd(Y,mult(X,X)),X),mult(Y,ld(Y,Z)))
= { by axiom 1 }
  ld(mult(rd(Y,mult(X,X)),X),Z)
